2026.11.10 晚报

覆盖窗口：2026-11-09T21:30:00+08:00 → 2026-11-10T21:30:00+08:00 · 更新时间：2026.11.10 21:56 北京时间 · 298 条

窗口：2026-11-09 21:30 至 2026-11-10 21:30（北京时间，UTC+8）｜监控名单 134 个账号｜所有时间均为北京时间。

Executive Summary｜核心结论

本期窗口为 2026-11-09 21:30 至 2026-11-10 21:30（北京时间，UTC+8）。其中 11 月 9 日 21:30 至 10 日 06:00 与本日早报窗口重叠，Fable 5.2 发布与黄仁勋事件的董事会调查已在早报出现；本期增量主线在美东周一交易时段及亚洲周二下午：（1）Anthropic IPO 路演条款落地、估值较夏季预期大幅下修，供应商以 IPO 价认购约 260 亿美元、约占新股 30%，S-1 同时披露短期算力租赁在三季度由盈转亏；（2）出口管制与“远程访问”漏洞立法在黄仁勋事件后据报更易推进，台媒出现 Nvidia 削减台积电订单的传闻，但月营收数据与部分卖方予以否定；（3）台积电 10 月营收与台湾半导体出口再创新高，Google 扩容 TPU 8tx/8i Max，定制硅生态对 NVDA 的长期竞争叙事加强；（4）AI 基建的信用约束（短贷 refinancing、利差走阔）与电力长约（核电）的不对称风险更加清晰，软银财报电话会否认“违约取消轮机订单”。以下“分析/含义”为本摘要的综合判断；凡标注“据报/传闻/作者观点”的内容均来自 X 帖子或其转述的媒体报道，未经独立核实，数字均以原帖为准。

一、Anthropic：IPO 定价下修 + 供应商认购 260 亿美元，S-1 暴露短期算力对利润的冲击

事实层（转述，未经独立核实）：@kimmonismus 称 Anthropic 已正式启动拟议 IPO 路演，拟发行 8.25 亿股、定价 70–78 美元，完全稀释估值约 1.24–1.38 万亿美元，低于此前约 1.5 万亿美元的预期。@ScroogeCap 梳理估值路径：8 月约 2.0–2.2 万亿（约 30x run rate）→ 10 月中 1.4–1.5 万亿（约 22–23x）→ 现市场共识靠近 1 万亿，认为是为避免类似 $SPCX 的上市后抛压、向 5 月融资轮估值靠拢。@ScroogeCap 另转述路透（引备案）称希望募资 693 亿美元。@Loris_Dalleau / @nodiligence：七家供应商按 IPO 价认购约 260 亿美元（谷歌、Nvidia 各 100 亿，AMD 25 亿，Broadcom、Micron、微软各 10 亿，台积电 5 亿），约占新发行股份的 30%。@nodiligence 进一步援引合同敞口：谷歌在 2033 年前至少有 1213 亿美元云容量应付（不论是否使用），另预期 2028 年起再向其采购 1250–1500 亿美元 TPU；Broadcom 租约应收 1612 亿美元；微软至 2033 年至少 472 亿美元；亚马逊未参与此次认购，但 4 月协议已允许后续最多再向其出售 150 亿美元股票。按其中位价约 74 美元，股权价值约 9680 亿美元（计入全部期权/授予则约 1.33 万亿），与 5 月一轮约 9650 亿美元接近；供应商股份不在承销商锁定期内（出售仍需注册）。

S-1 算力（@jukan05 转述）：短期租用“目前”显著贵于多年期合约，三季度 50 亿美元、前九个月 65 亿美元；营业利润从二季度盈利 9.70 亿美元转为三季度亏损 5.70 亿美元，公司归因于 50 亿美元短期算力与 20 亿美元非现金捐赠匹配费用。9 月末短期合约最低付款约 174 亿美元，10 月后再签约 66 亿，合计约 240 亿美元，约占未来 12 个月应付 707 亿美元的三分之一。作者估算：剔除短期容量后，三季度算力成本 48.6 亿美元（低于二季度 55.6 亿），收入/算力从 2.08 美元降至 1.72 美元——约 54 亿美元的收入增量主要由短期容量承接。公司称 Anthropic Direct（直接租芯片并在专用数据中心托管）上线后，将用更便宜的长期算力替换需求高峰期的短期采购。产品侧（重叠时段及本日评测）：Fable 5.2 已发布（8/40 美元每百万 token，较 5.1 低 20%）；@SemiAnalysis_ 的 GDPx 显示其在芯片设计/软件工程有个位数到十几分的提升，但认为这是“5.2 而非 5.5 量级”的边际改进，且尽管单价降 20%，平均每任务成本较 5.1 升 31%（67.16→88.15 美元）。

含义：IPO 的定价锚已从“2 万亿成长故事”回到接近 5 月已成交轮次；供应商认购降低了发行失败风险，但也意味着公开市场投资者与供应商的激励并不对称——后者的主要回报在长期算力合同，股票是“廉价门票”（260 亿不足其已承诺支出约 9009 亿美元的 3%）。对投资的直接含义：若 ANT 破发，谷歌/Nvidia 的浮亏相对合同现金流较小，公开发行买方则只有股票。利润率方面，S-1 把“模型需求超过长期合约容量 → 向 SpaceX 等支付高溢价短期算力 → 经营利润转负”写成了可验证的财务机制；跟踪 Direct 上线进度、2027 年 3 月后是否仍愿支付短期溢价，以及发行后锁定期外供应商的减持节奏。

二、出口管制与 Nvidia：游说效力下降、远程访问漏洞、砍单传闻对上游的再分配

事实/转述：@jukan05 引 The Information：Nvidia 长期游说反对限制中国公司通过海外云获取其最新芯片；黄仁勋事件后，该游说在华盛顿“分量下降”，三份法案更易推进——MATCH Act（限制光刻设备对华出口）、Overwatch Act（加强国会对商务部批准对华等国 AI 芯片销售的监督）、Chip Security Act（要求 AI 芯片内置追踪）。国防授权法预计在新一届民主党主导国会 1 月开会前通过，共和党领导层有动力现在把内容写入最终文本。更意外的是，白宫据报要求国会加入条款，责成商务部制定规则阻断对特定芯片的远程访问——这是白宫立场的转变；若通过，GPU 现货价格也可能承压。@AndrewCurran_ 称中期选举结果已使更严出口管制几乎成为定局，黄仁勋事件使之“更加确定”，并引 Leo Schwartz：也在讨论关闭远程访问漏洞，“Dario 想要的几乎都在实现”。@WisemanCap：BofA 将 LRCX、MU、NVDA 移出 US 1 List，评级仍为买入。

供应链传闻（需标注）：@jukan05 / @dnystedt 转述台媒及供应链传闻称，香港 H200 堆积 + 出口管制趋严，使 Nvidia 削减台积电订单的可能性上升；若发生，空出的晶圆与 CoWoS/高端探针卡产能可能流向 AMD 与 ASIC（Google TPU、AWS Trainium 等）。@jukan05 称自己也听过类似传闻、尚待向信源确认，并认为部分媒体像在重复传言。@FundaAI 明确将其视为纯猜测：即便 Rubin 因 HBM 技术问题略有延期，他们不认为 Nvidia 已削减对台积电的订单。@SKundojjala / @dnystedt 给出的 10 月营收（见下节）与“需求正在崩溃”的叙事并不一致。@jasonschips 称已有拟议股东集体诉讼，指 Nvidia 与黄仁勋隐瞒其与疑似中国间谍的关系（指控而非裁定）。

含义：对 NVDA 的核心变量仍是“中国相关需求（含东南亚云租用）在立法中被切掉多少”，而不是 CEO 去留。远程访问条款若落地，冲击的是 GPU 租赁/现货和海外 neocloud 的利用率，而非台积电的总体负荷——后者在砍单传闻下反而可能出现客户再分配。在证据层级上，The Information 的立法进展 > 台媒砍单传闻；在验证砍单前，不应把订单下修写成事实。跟踪：NDAA 文本、商务部远程访问规则、H200 许可与香港库存去化。

三、需求侧并未转弱：台积电 10 月纪录、台湾出口、Google TPU 生态与 Infineon 电力半导体

台积电与出口（公司披露/法定月报，数字以原帖为准）：@dnystedt：10 月营收 NT$5868.9 亿（约 184.6 亿美元，按公司四季度汇率指引 1 美元=31.8 新台币），同比 +59.7%，刷新 8 月 NT$5148.1 亿的纪录；1–10 月营收同比 +43.3% 至 NT$4.49 万亿，仍跟踪全年约 44% 增长指引；四季度指引 523–543 亿美元。@SKundojjala 按美元口径：10 月约 182.36 亿美元，环比 +13%（高于 2021–25 年 10 月平均 +10.5%），连续第 8 个历史新高月、也是首次单月超过 170 亿和 180 亿美元；1–10 月 1411 亿美元、同比 +40.1%，已比 2025 年全年多 186 亿美元；若 11–12 月持平，2026 年约 1776 亿美元（+45%），但历史上 11–12 月环比下降，要完成 44% 指引需要好于季节性。@dnystedt：台湾 10 月半导体出口同比 +63.0% 至 311.7 亿美元，1–10 月 2511.4 亿美元、+49.1%；整体出口 +58.9% 至纪录 967.6 亿美元。

Google TPU：@SemiAnalysis_ / @dnystedt / @TradexWhisperer：Google 在第八代 TPU 组合中新增 TPU 8tx（在 8t 上加硬件机密计算，用于小规模训练与安全推理）与 TPU 8i Max（两颗 8i 封装在一起）。8i Max 的 HBM 带宽 8.6→17.2 TB/s（2 倍）、容量 288→384 GB；公司称一年内推出四个 TPU 系统尚属首次。@PhotonCap：AMD 宣布 EPYC 将为 TPU 8tx 主机供电；Broadcom 合作覆盖未来 TPU 代际与网络至 2031；Marvell 也加入定制硅生态——作者认为 Google 不只是在做 Nvidia 的替代，而是在搭一整条 AI 半导体生态，长期最有趣的竞争对手可能是 Google 自己。@SemiAnalysis_ 还称在推理栈上“TPUv7 已明显优于 GB300 NVL72”（其评测观点，非第三方审计）。

Infineon（@SKundojjala / @dnystedt）：2026 财年营收创纪录 163.5 亿欧元、+12%；2027 财年指引约 200 亿欧元（+20%），毛利率指引中高 40% 区间；数据中心 2027 财年指引 40 亿欧元（此前 AI 单项曾指引 >25 亿，口径有调整）；AI 电力 2026 财年 17 亿欧元（指引 >16 亿）、同比翻倍以上。产能预订协议累计收入已超 100 亿欧元；碳化硅 2026 财年 7 亿、+10%，2027 财年指引远高于 10 亿欧元；2027 年资本开支 30–35 亿欧元。拟将 NOR Flash/F-RAM 以 11.2 亿美元出售给华邦电，预计 2027 日历年后半年交割，可贡献最多约 10 亿欧元自由现金流。@semivision_tw：英飞凌称已在奥地利 Villach 全尺寸晶圆厂环境中，用 12 吋碳化硅晶圆做出功能完整的功率器件，并称是全球首家。

含义：至少在晶圆厂和台湾出口这个“已发生的需求”层面，10 月没有看到 AI 建设周期断裂；这与股价/信贷对 2027–28 年电力与许可的担忧可以同时成立。TPU 8i Max 把瓶颈明确指向 HBM 带宽/容量，利好 $MU / SK 海力士等，也强化 AVGO/AMD/MRVL 在 Google 体系中的份额故事。Infineon 把“AI 电力”从叙事写成了可指引的收入项（2027 年数据中心 40 亿欧元），是对电力电子、而非 GPU 本身的需求确认。

四、AI 基建融资变贵、电力长约更“安全”；软银否认轮机订单违约

信用（@nodiligence，作者整理的交易地图与公开报道，非官方统计）：今年进入 AI 债务的每条路径都更挑剔，感受最深的是必须为每座新集群获得贷款人同意的 GPU 云。8–10 月，以数据中心租约为支持的单 A 债券较国债利差 257bp（一季度 195bp），每 10 亿美元债务约多付 620 万美元/年；重复发行人 Switch 从 3 月 225bp 升至 10 月 290bp。hyperscaler 十年期利差今年走阔约 50bp（优质工业约 20bp），今年已发行约 2960 亿美元债券，超过高盛原估 2500 亿，高盛预计 2027 年超过 4000 亿。Nebius 放弃高收益债、转借花旗 35 亿美元过桥（SOFR+2.75%，每 90 天上调 25bp，至 2027 年 7 月 3.5%；若全额支取，最后一档较首档一年多付约 2625 万美元），到期 2027 年 10 月 16 日。其长文还称：债券市场看起来平静，是因为大部分建设还在等待“建成再换债券”的短期银行贷款里；Oracle Project Jupiter 贷款据英国《金融时报》报价 89–91 美分，Anthropic 德州 156 亿美元过桥对应的 50–70 亿美元债券被搁置；在电力和许可把 refinancing 日期往后推时，风险留在日本和法国等“持有型”银行，再通过风险转移卖给已持有同一栋楼股权的基金。作者强调这不是违约，而是日期风险。

电力与海外融资：@nodiligence 以 Constellation（$CEG）为例——亚马逊从 Calvert Cliffs 锁定 690MW、20 年；谷歌为 11 座核电机组的 uprate 签 20 年，另签 2700MW、15 年；Meta 服务器按 5.5 年折旧，2026 年买的服务器约 2032 年已摊完，而供电合同可跑到 2046。Uprate 还可获能源部支持、最高 50 亿美元、30 年、国债+0.375% 的贷款。若 AI 需求降温，发电商继续收款，hyperscaler 承担“芯片已摊完、电还要再买十几年”的合约。另：沙特国家基础设施基金 11 月 8 日与 Humain 签署 25 亿里亚尔（约 6.67 亿美元）贷款——最多 11 亿里亚尔（约 2.93 亿）用于约 25MW IT 负载的 GPU，最多 14 亿里亚尔（约 3.73 亿）用于按最高 1GW 设计的变电站与并网；Humain 自 11 月 1 日起为 PIF 51% / 阿美 49%，阿美 2026 年将注入约 99 亿里亚尔现金。作者认为真正风险是其余 975MW 的填租速度，且风险留在国家。

软银：@aleabitoreddit / @ScroogeCap 引财报电话会——有分析师问及马斯克“软银正在对其轮机订单违约”的帖子，CFO 后藤芳光称“这完全不正确，不存在此类违约”；同时呼应 $GE 首席执行官 Scott Strazik 称未见采购取消或交易对手融资困难。这是对一周前市场传闻的公司层面否认，但原帖也提醒仍需看是否有未披露的细节。

含义：AI 建设的股权故事和信用故事已经分叉——需求（台积电、出口、电力半导体）仍强，但“谁愿意把一年期建造贷款接成长期债券”在收紧，利好已锁定长电价/核电合约的发电商（$CEG 等）和能在自己资产负债表上等待的 hyperscaler，不利于依赖过桥、项目抵押和担保的 neocloud / 开发商。软银否定轮机违约，短期减轻 $GEV 与软银融资链上的传闻冲击，但不改变电力设备交付仍是 2027–28 年约束的判断。

五、存储与 HBM：技术路线（HBM5）清晰，估值与名单调整反映的是情绪而非已证实的周期反转

技术：@SemiAnalysis_ 根据三星 Hot Chips 2026：HBM5 保持与 HBM4E 相同的 2048 引脚，单引脚速率从 14 Gbps 提到超过 28 Gbps，带宽约翻倍；因 DRAM bank 无法简单提速一倍，作者估计每层 bank 数约翻倍，TSV 也加倍，PHY 移到基片边缘、到 GPU 的路径从约 5.5mm 缩到约 2mm。代价是 HBM5 PHY 功耗可超过 HBM4E 的 4 倍，供应商引入位于 PHY 上方的热“烟囱”。这是带宽继续翻倍、功耗与散热成为下一瓶颈的路线图，而非需求消失。关键词抽样中，@carrioresearch 用一条供应链视频把 HBM 描述为“争夺同一批 DRAM 晶圆”的战争（SK 海力士/三星/美光晶圆 → 基片 → 12 高堆叠 → 测试 → 台积电 CoWoS 进入 Rubin，每颗 288GB）。@StragglerLiu 指出：2027 年更多 DRAM 晶圆不等于更多商品 DRAM 供给，因为美光已披露 HBM 对 DDR5 大约 3:1 的产能置换比，且新一代 HBM 该比率还在升。

定价与名单：@WisemanCap 记录 BofA 将 MU（以及 LRCX、NVDA）移出最高确信名单、维持买入。关键词抽样中 @JJoonWick 认为这对三星/SK 海力士估值有情绪传导，并提醒与摩根士丹利关于 2028 年存储盈利“悬崖”的警告对照，但强调这不是降级、也不是周期见顶的证据。@antfeedapp 称三星、SK 海力士远期市盈率约 3.1x 和 2.6x，处 2013 年以来估值底部区域，并称渠道检查在股价见顶数月后仍未见砍单（推广性质内容，需折扣）。@BullTheoryio 转述高盛认为韩股存储“可能翻倍”，称市场在用 2027 年盈利打七折定价，并转述 SK 海力士称 DRAM/HBM 至 2029 年基本售罄、三星称 2027 年计划 HBM 产量已售罄——上述销量/营收数字来自低或中等关注账号的二次转述，未独立核实。

含义：HBM5 说明存储商的资本开支与产品组合会继续偏向高带宽、高功耗堆叠，商品 DRAM 供给被“置换”的逻辑对 2027 年价格有支撑；BofA 调出确信名单是仓位和相对吸引力的信号，不是基本面反转。把 2–3x 远期盈利当成“已经计入崩盘”可以讨论，但证据仍然是订单与售罄表述，而非已公布的砍单。跟踪：美光/海力士资本开支指引、HBM 与 DDR5 晶圆分配、以及电网瓶颈是否在 2027 上半年转化为订单放缓（早报已出现的卖方观点，本期未见新的硬数据打脸或坐实）。

#8 @SemiAnalysis_ · 2026-11-10 13:28 北京时间

要点：🚨 HBM5 在不增加任何引脚的情况下把带宽翻倍 🚨

HBM5 保持与 HBM4E 相同的 2048 个引脚，但把每个引脚跑得快了将近 2 倍。三星在 Hot Chips 2026 的报告显示，引脚速率从 HBM4E 的 14 Gbps 提升到 HBM5 的超过 28 Gbps。

更快的引脚只是故事的一半。为了喂饱每个引脚，引脚背后的一切也要加倍：DRAM bank、TSV，以及基片上升级的 PHY。
下面用超市来解释这是怎么工作的 🛒（1/6）

为何重要：HBM5 在不增加引脚的前提下把带宽再翻倍，并指出 PHY 功耗与散热成为新瓶颈——直接关系 2027–28 年 $MU / 海力士 / 三星的产品组合与资本开支。

【二级市场投资人】

@nodiligence（保留 8 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 13 条）

2026-11-09 21:34 北京时间 ｜ Source: @nodiligence ｜ 原帖链接

$GOOGL

开发商最好的资产是一块有电的场地，而 Google 刚刚表明，它宁愿早点把场地拿过来，也不愿意付给开发商租金。

Crusoe 在怀俄明州夏延附近的园区上花了一年多：土地、许可、与隔壁 Tallgrass 的供电方案。Google 本应是租户。它不喜欢成本和进度，6 月把 Crusoe 挤出局，现在拥有该场地并计划投入 170 亿美元建设。

所以 Crusoe 拿到了一次性的前期工作报酬，失去了它指望的多年租金（Google 支付的价格未披露）。

我认为每个只有一个超大租户的开发商都面临同样的上限，因为租户的借款成本比开发商低，一旦开发商在成本或工期上出现差池，买下场地就比等待更划算。

Crusoe 在 9 月以 309 亿美元估值融资 39 亿美元，那时 Google 已经离开。下一个考验是它为 Microsoft 在阿比林建设的 900MW 园区，首栋建筑预计 2027 年中交付。

如果那个项目延误，Microsoft 会不会像 Google 那样做？

原文：$GOOGL

a developer's best asset is a site with power on it, and google just showed it'll take the site sooner than pay the developer rent on it.

crusoe spent more than a year on the campus near cheyenne, wyoming: the land, the permits, a power plan with tallgrass next door. google was supposed to be the tenant. it didn't like the cost or the schedule, pushed crusoe out in june, and now owns the site and plans to spend $17bn building it.

so crusoe gets paid once for the groundwork and loses the years of rent it was counting on (the price google paid wasn't disclosed).

i think every developer with one giant tenant is capped the same way, because the tenant borrows cheaper than the developer does, so the moment the developer slips on cost or timing, buying the site beats waiting for it.

crusoe raised $3.9bn at a $30.9bn value in september, after google had already walked. the next test is the 900MW campus it's building for microsoft in abilene, first building due mid 2027.

if that one misses, does microsoft do what google did?

2026-11-10 00:02 北京时间 ｜ Source: @nodiligence ｜ 原帖链接

$NVDA $QCOM $FJTSY $ARM $AMZN $SKM

Nvidia 已经不再试图把机架里的每一颗芯片都卖给你，而是确保机架里的每一颗芯片都通过 Nvidia 来通信。

Rebellions 与 Nvidia 和 SK Telecom 签约，通过 NVLink Fusion（Nvidia 的芯片间互连，让 72 颗芯片像一颗大芯片一样工作）把其 R200（一款韩国推理芯片）接入 Nvidia GPU。他们将测试的方案是：Nvidia GPU 负责 prefill（读取整个提示，重数学运算），R200 负责 decode（逐 token 写出回答，主要在等待内存）。R200 要到 2028 年才推出。

Rebellions 并不是第一个接入这条链路的外部芯片：
——高通和富士通把服务器 CPU 接入它
——Arm 把它内置进授权给所有人的 CPU 设计
——亚马逊正在设计其自研 AI 芯片 Trainium4 以接入它
——Rebellions 现在承担 decode 那一半工作

而 Nvidia 自己今年对 decode 的解决方案是把 Rubin GPU 与 Groq LPX 机架配对，而不是一颗更好的 GPU。

我认为 Nvidia 已经接受，decode——每当 agent 多写一点就会增长的那一半——将流向不是 GPU 的更便宜芯片。拥有这根“线”，就是它在自己没有计算的 token 上仍然能收钱的方式。

所以当 R200 在 2028 年出现时，SK Telecom 最终是按 token 付给 Nvidia 更少的钱，还是为更少的 GPU 和一条无法替换的链路付同样的钱？

原文：$NVDA $QCOM $FJTSY $ARM $AMZN $SKM

nvidia has stopped trying to sell you every chip in the rack. it's making sure every chip in the rack talks through nvidia instead.

rebellions signed with nvidia and SK telecom to wire its R200 (a korean inference chip) into nvidia GPUs over NVLink fusion (nvidia's chip-to-chip link, the thing that lets 72 chips act like one big one). the plan they're going to test: the nvidia GPU does prefill (reading the whole prompt, heavy maths) and the R200 does decode (writing the answer one token at a time, which mostly sits waiting on memory). the R200 isn't due until 2028.

rebellions isn't the first outside chip on that link:
-- qualcomm and fujitsu connect their server CPUs to it
-- arm built it into the CPU designs it licenses to everyone else
-- amazon is designing trainium4, its own AI chip, to sit on it
-- rebellions now takes the decode half of the job

and nvidia's own fix for decode this year was pairing rubin GPUs with groq LPX racks, not a better GPU.

i think nvidia has accepted that decode, the half that grows every time an agent writes more, is going to cheaper chips that aren't GPUs. owning the wire is how it still gets paid on a token it didn't compute.

so when the R200 shows up in 2028, does SK telecom end up paying nvidia less per token, or the same money for fewer GPUs and a link it can't swap out?

2026-11-10 14:18 北京时间 ｜ Source: @nodiligence ｜ 原帖链接

$NBIS $CRWV $META $GOOG

今年进入 AI 债务的每一条路径，贷款人都变得更挑剔；我认为感受最深的是 GPU 云，因为每建一座新集群都需要贷款人点头。

价格怎么动的：

—— 以数据中心租约为支持的单 A 债券，8–10 月较国债利差 257bp，一季度是 195bp。每 10 亿美元债务，一年大约多付 620 万美元。

—— 重复发行人 Switch 从 3 月的 225bp 升到 10 月的 290bp，所以不能把变化全解释成“上市的是哪些交易变了”。

—— hyperscaler 十年期利差今年走阔约 50bp，优质工业公司大约只走阔 20bp。他们今年已经发行约 2960 亿美元债券，超过高盛原先 2500 亿美元的估计，高盛预计 2027 年超过 4000 亿美元。

—— 摩根大通对“产生收入的不动产”（其备案称包括数据中心）的敞口，9 月末达到 294 亿美元，占其房地产敞口的 12.6%，三年前是 8%。

hyperscaler 也要付得更多，但背后是整个公司在撑。Nebius 9 月看过高收益债，10 月放弃，转借花旗 35 亿美元过桥，每 90 天更贵一次，直到 2027 年 7 月。

以我近来看到的情况，这对任何出租 GPU 的人来说，就是未来一年的样子：有担保、期限短、更贵。

原文：$NBIS $CRWV $META $GOOG

lenders got pickier on every route into AI debt this year, and i think the companies that feel it most are the GPU clouds, because they need a lender's yes for every new cluster.

where the price moved:

-- single-A bonds backed by data-centre leases paid 257bp over treasuries from august to october, against 195bp in the first quarter. on $1bn of debt that's about $6.2m a year more.

-- switch, a repeat borrower, went from 225bp in march to 290bp in october, so a change in which deals came to market can't explain all of it.

-- hyperscaler 10-year spreads widened about 50bp this year against about 20bp for strong industrial companies. they've already sold about $296bn of bonds this year, past goldman's $250bn estimate, and goldman sees more than $400bn in 2027.

-- jpmorgan's exposure to income-producing property, a category its filing says includes data centres, reached $29.4bn at the end of september, 12.6% of its real estate exposure against 8% three years earlier.

the hyperscalers pay more too, but the whole company stands behind them. nebius looked at a high-yield bond in september, dropped it in october and took a $3.5bn citi bridge that gets more expensive every 90 days until july 2027.

from what i'm seeing lately, that's what the next year looks like for anyone renting out GPUs: secured, short-dated and more expensive.

$CEG

眼下 AI 里最安全的钱是电力合同，Constellation 这一季显示这种不对称已经有多明显。

hyperscaler 签下的电，会在芯片已经折旧完很久之后还在付：
—— 亚马逊：Calvert Cliffs 690MW、20 年
—— 谷歌：11 座核电机组 uprate（从现有反应堆挤出的额外出力）签 20 年，外加 2700MW、15 年

Meta 的服务器按 5.5 年折旧。2026 年买的一台服务器大约 2032 年就从账上消失，而把它启动的电力合同会跑到 2046 年。

这些 uprate 还可获得能源部支持、最高 50 亿美元、30 年、国债+0.375% 的贷款。

如果 AI 需求降温，Constellation 继续收款，hyperscaler 则要为不再运行的芯片承担 14 年电力。

原文：$CEG

the safest money in AI right now is the power contract, and constellation's quarter shows how lopsided it's got.

the hyperscalers are signing for electricity they'll still be paying for long after the chips are written off:
-- amazon: 690MW from calvert cliffs for 20 years
-- google: 20 years on the uprates (extra output squeezed out of existing reactors) at 11 nuclear units, plus 2,700MW for 15 years

meta writes its servers off over 5.5 years. so a server bought in 2026 is off the books by about 2032, and the power deal that switched it on runs to 2046.

and those uprates are getting a DOE-backed loan of up to $5bn for 30 years at 0.375% over treasuries.

if AI demand cools, constellation keeps getting paid and the hyperscaler carries 14 years of power it bought for chips it no longer runs.

2026-11-10 19:33 北京时间 ｜ Source: @nodiligence ｜ 原帖链接

[X Article] The risk in AI data-centre debt is a refinancing date nobody can see
［X Article：《AI 数据中心债务的风险，是一个谁也看不见的再融资日期》长文要点，非逐字全文］债券市场看起来仍平静，作者认为是因为大部分 AI 建设还没进入债券，而在“建成后再换成债券”的短期银行贷款里。hyperscaler 可以发股或借 30 年，项目公司大约只借一年、必须有人在之后接走。

11 月 6 日美国投资级利差收于 0.93%、高收益 3.42%。hyperscaler 十年期今年走阔约 50bp（优质工业约 20bp）；5 年期单 A 数据中心债 8–10 月较国债 257bp（一季度 195bp）。Oracle 11 月 2 日新债票息 7%–8.65%。近两个月三笔最大建设贷款“卡住”：Jupiter 贷款据英国《金融时报》报价 89–91 美分（银行转售不出去）；Anthropic 德州 156 亿美元过桥对应的 50–70 亿美元债券被搁置；摩根大通与三菱日联为两个 Oracle 相关项目安排 380 亿美元建设贷花费数月。

在一座楼有电、有租户之前，外部看不到已提款多少、贷款值多少、由谁持有，债券只给已经建成并卖掉的部分定价。真正的风险是退出日期：电力和许可不断把日期往后推。作者自 9 月起手工追踪，称现有 74 国 1793 个站点、956 笔融资。美元贷款中 115 笔、合计 2960 亿美元已签署/关闭但无公开提款额，债券和 ABS 有 1750 亿美元现金可见。KKR 估计公募投资级买家大约只能覆盖 AI 建设 1.7 万亿美元，其余约 6.3 万亿（约 79%）要来自私募债务、资产支持和基础设施债。

Nexus（Anthropic 德州园区）是 142 亿美元一年期贷款、七家银行约各 20 亿；计划用 9 月高收益债偿还一部分。谷歌只在数据中心建成后才为 Anthropic 的租金和电费背书。德州在 ERCOT 完成全州审计前暂停数据中心许可，审计报告拟于 12 月 10 日提交监管。9 月 18–28 日：Jupiter 贷款折价、德州暂停许可、Oracle 向 Blue Owl 开发单元发不可抗力通知（租金或可推迟最多三年）、Nexus 债券搁置。作者强调这不是违约，而是日期风险；贷款人按 Oracle 评级给规模，但不可抗力把“以为是 Oracle 的风险”送回贷款人。

Alphabet 三季度筹资约 410 亿美元，其中 124 亿为新股、未回购；Meta 10 月 26 日定价约 375 亿美元新股本；Oracle 至 8 月当季 ATM 出售 200 亿美元股票，11 月 2 日再各 100 亿股票和债券。微软 9 月末有 3646 亿美元尚未起租的数据中心租赁，部分要到 2027–2034 财年、且须满足合同条件才开始。彭博 11 月 6 日称，在德州、俄亥俄、佐治亚、密歇根和新墨西哥，竞选时主张停止或暂停数据中心建设的州长当选，美国待建容量约 40% 位于这些州。

作者认为卡住的贷款主要落在日资和法资“持有型”银行（三菱日联同时出现在 Jupiter、Oracle-Vantage 和 Nexus）；风险转移（SRT）的买方往往已持有同一资产的股权，风险并未分散、只是更不透明。未来 90 天若出现：日法银行披露的数据中心提款敞口其实很小、Nexus 债券按时定价、或德州/新墨西哥给出明确许可日期，他会修正判断。作者声明为独立研究，并非投资建议。

$ANT $GOOGL $NVDA $AMD $MSFT $AVGO $MU $TSM $AMZN

Anthropic 把 IPO 价格砍了一半，而且一部分对价来自它采购算力的那些公司。如果你第一天就进场，应该再想一想价格会怎么走。

七家供应商按 IPO 价认购 260 亿美元股票，约占 Anthropic 新发行股份的 30%。

—— 谷歌和 Nvidia 各投 100 亿美元，
—— AMD 25 亿美元，
—— Broadcom、美光和微软各 10 亿美元，
—— 台积电 5 亿美元。

再看看它们对 Anthropic 意味着什么。

—— 谷歌在 2033 年前至少有 1213 亿美元云容量应收（不论是否使用），Anthropic 还预期 2028 年起再向其采购 1250–1500 亿美元 TPU。
—— Broadcom 投了 10 亿，同时对租给 Anthropic 的设备有 1612 亿美元租金应收。
—— 微软在 2033 年前至少有 472 亿美元应收。AMD 的 25 亿计入它在一笔硬件协议里承诺的 50 亿美元股票，而 Anthropic 预期该协议下支出超过 200 亿美元，所以买股票本身就是芯片销售的一部分。
—— 亚马逊至少有 1100 亿美元应收，却完全没参与。它大概也不需要：4 月协议已允许 Anthropic 在新算力上线时，按 30 日均价再向其出售最多 150 亿美元股票。

这就是为什么估值砍半也没挡住它们。10 月初投资者把 Anthropic 看到最高 2 万亿美元。今天给出的区间，按 74 美元中位价约 9680 亿美元（若计入全部期权和授予约 1.33 万亿），预计下周开始交易。供应商仍按更低价格买，是因为它们的钱从来不是从股票里来的。对其他所有人来说，这次下调多半只是噪音。

5 月那一轮是 9650 亿美元，所以真正付过钱的人并没有亏，只有写过 2 万亿的人在账面上“亏了”。

对供应商这是一张廉价门票。260 亿美元不足 Anthropic 已承诺支出 9009 亿美元的 3%，而且它们的股份不在承销商锁定期内（出售仍需注册）。

如果 ANT 跌破 74 美元，谷歌和 Nvidia 在持股上会有一点浮亏，但合同还在收款。旁边的公开发行买方手里只有股票。

原文：$ANT $GOOGL $NVDA $AMD $MSFT $AVGO $MU $TSM $AMZN

anthropic halved its IPO price and is also partly paid for by the companies it buys compute from, and if you are going in at day 1, you should think twice what the price action would be.

seven suppliers are taking $26bn of stock at the IPO price, about 30% of the new shares anthropic sells.

-- google and nvidia put in $10bn each,
-- AMD $2.5bn,
-- broadcom, micron and microsoft $1bn each,
-- TSMC $500m.

and look who they are to anthropic.

-- google is owed at least $121.3bn for cloud capacity through 2033, used or not, and anthropic expects to pay it another $125bn to $150bn for TPUs from 2028.
-- broadcom put in $1bn and is owed $161.2bn of rent on the equipment it leases anthropic.
-- microsoft is owed at least $47.2bn through 2033. AMD's $2.5bn counts toward the $5bn of stock it promised inside a hardware deal anthropic expects to spend over $20bn on, so the stock buy is part of the chip sale.
-- amazon is owed at least $110bn and isn't in it at all. it probably doesn't need to be. its april deal already lets anthropic sell it up to $15bn more stock later, at the 30-day average price, as new compute comes online.

which is why the valuation getting halved hasn't put any of them off. in early october investors were putting anthropic at up to $2tn. the range it set today comes to about $968bn at the $74 midpoint (about $1.33tn if you count every option and grant), and it's expected to start trading next week. the suppliers are still buying at the lower price because the stock was never where their money comes from. for everyone else the cut is mostly noise btw.

the may round was $965bn, so nobody who actually paid is down, only the people who wrote $2tn.

for the suppliers this is a cheap ticket. $26bn is under 3% of the $900.9bn anthropic has committed to spend, and their shares aren't in the underwriters' lockup (they still need registration to sell).

if ANT trades under $74, google and nvidia take a small hit on the stake and keep collecting on the contracts. the public buyer next to them only has the stock.

2026-11-10 18:43 北京时间 ｜ Source: @nodiligence ｜ 原帖链接

沙特国家基础设施基金 11 月 8 日与 Humain 签署 25 亿里亚尔（约 6.67 亿美元）贷款，我的猜测是国家在租户到位之前就先为电网放贷。

—— 最多 11 亿里亚尔（约 2.93 亿美元）用于约 25MW IT 负载的 GPU。

—— 最多 14 亿里亚尔（约 3.73 亿美元）用于按最高 1GW 设计的变电站与并网。

—— 利率、期限、担保和租户均未披露。

Humain 自 11 月 1 日起为 PIF 51%、阿美 49%，阿美 2026 年将注入约 99 亿里亚尔（约 26 亿美元）现金，大约是两笔贷款合计的四倍。所以我认为 Humain 大部分芯片自己出资，基础设施基金的钱真正用在电网上。

利率未披露，下限是三个月 SAIBOR（8 月 4.83%，9 月 5.01%–5.54%）。14 亿里亚尔全额的利息一年约 6800 万至 7800 万里亚尔（1800–2100 万美元），按 25MW 计每 MWh IT 容量约 82–94 美元，按满负荷 1GW 则约 2 美元。

挂牌 3.90 美元/小时的 H200，每 MWh 约赚 3060 美元；按已备案一年期交易均价 1.67 美元则约 1310 美元，所以即便只有 25MW，利息也只占租金的 3%–7%。

真正的风险是其余 975MW 填租有多慢；国家既是股东又是贷款人，这笔风险留在国家。

原文：saudi arabia's national infrastructure fund signed up to SAR 2.5bn ($667m) of loans with humain on 8 november, and my guess is the state is lending for grid well ahead of its tenants.

-- up to SAR 1.1bn ($293m) for GPUs for about 25MW of IT load.

-- up to SAR 1.4bn ($373m) for substations and grid links designed for up to 1GW.

-- no rate, tenor, security or tenant disclosed.

humain has been 51% PIF and 49% aramco since 1 november, and aramco is contributing about SAR 9.9bn ($2.6bn) in cash during 2026, roughly four times both loans together. so i think humain funds most of the chips itself and infra's money is really about the grid.

with no rate disclosed, the floor is three-month SAIBOR, 4.83% in august and 5.01% to 5.54% in september. interest alone on the full SAR 1.4bn is SAR 68m to 78m ($18m to $21m) a year, about $82 to $94 per MWh of IT capacity at 25MW and about $2 at the full 1GW.

H200s listed at $3.90 an hour earn about $3,060 per MWh, or about $1,310 at the $1.67 filed one-year deals averaged, so even at 25MW the interest is 3% to 7% of the rent.

the genuine risk here is how slowly the other 975MW would fill, and with the state as owner and lender, that risk stays with the state.

@GavinSBaker（保留 5 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 10 条）

2026-11-10 05:43 北京时间 ｜ Source: @GavinSBaker ｜ 原帖链接

有件事值得那些认为拜登和特朗普两届政府允许向中国出口性能较弱 GPU 是错误的人更多讨论：

自 2025 年夏天以来，越来越多是*中国*、而不只是华盛顿在阻止 Nvidia GPU 进入中国。在商务部长 Lutnick 说目标是让中国开发者对美国技术栈“上瘾”后的几周内，北京就开始施压本国科技巨头停止购买 H20。随后又命令它们停止购买 RTX Pro 6000D，派海关团队到港口打击 Nvidia 芯片进口，并禁止新的国家资助数据中心使用外国 AI 芯片。而截至上周，据路透社报道，约 60 万颗 H200 滞留在香港，因为北京不批准大规模进口一款仍然优于中国本土产业大规模生产的任何产品的芯片。

如果允许出口这些较弱的 GPU 是一个*有利于*中国的政策错误，那为什么阻止它们的是*中国*？

在我看来，中国明白：如果它依赖美国 AI 硬件——无论是在中国本土，还是通过各种东南亚云（包括美国最大 hyperscaler 在东南亚的可用区）远程访问——那么在未来任何冲突中我们都拥有巨大的筹码。能够瞬间关闭云，实际上才是最大的筹码。

是的，中国 AI 实验室仍然想要 Nvidia 芯片。过去一年 H200 服务器的灰色市场价格大约涨了两倍。但这恰恰说明了问题。北京愿意让本国 AI 实验室付出真实代价来避免依赖美国 GPU，这说明北京如何看待 GPU 进口：不是礼物，而是对其自身雄心的威胁。

也许，只是也许，向中国出售落后于我们最先进水平的 GPU，让中国开发者对美国 GPU 保持“上瘾”，使中国不去建立一个我们无法控制或影响、*且*中国可能随时间与我们的对手共享的替代 AI 硬件生态，其实是一项聪明的、亲美的国防政策。而让他们使用东南亚云可能是更聪明的政策，因为云很容易关闭。

我过去相信、现在仍然相信这是聪明的政策。而且我这么说时，我在这里的帖子对华鹰派到我都不会再去那里旅行了。

请注意，拜登和特朗普两届政府都出于上述原因允许出口性能较弱的 GPU。只是在特朗普政府时期，中国才意识到这项其实相当聪明、有利于国防的政策的风险，然后开始限制 GPU。

H20 的教训是，刻意阉割的芯片并不能让中国留在我们的技术栈上。北京之所以对 H20 和 RTX Pro 6000D 动手，是因为它判断国产芯片已经追上了它们。所以，如果中国发展出一个与俄罗斯、伊朗和朝鲜共享的替代 AI 硬件生态，我们回头会认为，在 2025 年向中国提供 B30A，而不是最终逐案批准的 H200，会更加明智。尤其是 H200 并非前沿：随着 Vera Rubin 已全面量产，它比美国实验室正在购买的产品落后两代。

总而言之，Jensen 一直在游说的立场被拜登*和*特朗普两届政府采纳，*而且*随着中国实验室越来越多地让其 MoE 架构适配国产硬件，这一立场每个月都显得更明智。据报道，DeepSeek 把 V4 的早期访问权给了华为和寒武纪而不是 Nvidia 和 AMD，此后一个华为主导的团队在昇腾芯片上对 V4-Pro 进行了后训练。

如果美国*真的*在 AI 上落后于中国，那些帮助加速了这一中国替代 AI 硬件生态诞生的美国对华鹰派，应该承担相当大一部分责任。

原文：Something that merits more discussion amongst those who believe it was a mistake by both the Biden and Trump administrations to allow the export of less powerful GPUs to China:

Since the summer of 2025, it has increasingly been *China*, not just DC, blocking Nvidia GPUs in China. Within weeks of Secretary Lutnick saying the goal was to get Chinese developers "addicted" to the American technology stack, Beijing began pressuring its tech giants to stop buying the H20. Then it ordered them to stop buying the RTX Pro 6000D, sent customs teams to the ports to crack down on Nvidia chip imports, and barred foreign AI chips from new state-funded data centers. And as of last week, Reuters reports that roughly 600,000 H200s are sitting in Hong Kong because Beijing won't approve large-scale imports of a chip that is still better than anything China's own industry makes at scale.

If allowing the export of these less powerful GPUs was a policy mistake that *advantaged* China, then why is *China* the one blocking them?

From my perspective, China understands that if they are dependent on American AI hardware - either on the ground in China or accessed remotely via the wide range of southeast Asian clouds (which include SE Asian availability zones from the largest American hyperscalers) - then we have immense leverage over them in any future conflict. Being able to turn off the clouds instantly is actually the greatest leverage.

Yes, Chinese AI labs still want Nvidia chips. Gray market prices for H200 servers have roughly tripled over the past year. But that is exactly the point. Beijing is willing to impose real costs on its own AI labs to avoid dependence on American GPUs, which tells you how Beijing views GPU imports: not as a gift, but as a threat to its own ambitions.

Maybe, just maybe, selling China GPUs that trail our own state of the art, so that Chinese developers stay “addicted” to American GPUs and China doesn't build an alternative AI hardware ecosystem that we have no control or influence over *and* that China could share over time with our adversaries, was actually a smart, pro-American national defense policy. And letting them access southeast Asian clouds might be an even smarter policy as so easy to turn clouds off.

I believed and still believed it was smart policy. And I say this as someone whose posts here are hawkish enough on China that I won't travel there anymore.

And note that both the Biden and Trump administration allowed the export of less powerful GPUs to China for all of these reasons. It was only under the Trump administration that China woke up to the risk of what was actually a pretty smart, pro-national defense policy and then China began restricting GPUs.

The lesson of the H20 is that a deliberately crippled chip doesn't keep China on our stack. Beijing moved against the H20 and RTX Pro 6000D as it concluded that domestic Chinese chips had caught up to them. So if China develops an alternative AI hardware ecosystem that it shares with Russia, Iran and North Korea, we will look back and think it would have been even smarter to offer China the B30A in 2025 instead of the H200s we eventually approved case-by-case. Especially given the H200 is not the frontier: with Vera Rubin now in full production, it is two generations behind what American labs are buying.

All of this is to say that Jensen has been consistently lobbying for a position that was adapted by both the Biden *and* Trump admins *and* looks smarter every month that goes by as Chinese labs increasingly adapt their MoE architectures to domestic Chinese hardware. DeepSeek reportedly gave Huawei and Cambricon early access to V4 instead of Nvidia and AMD, and a Huawei-led team has since post-trained V4-Pro on Ascend chips.

And if America *ever* falls behind China in AI, the China hawks here in America who helped accelerate the creation of this alternative Chinese AI hardware ecosystem will deserve a significant share of the blame.

2026-11-10 13:28 北京时间 ｜ Source: @SemiAnalysis_ ｜ 原帖链接

🚨 HBM5 在不增加任何引脚的情况下把带宽翻倍 🚨

HBM5 保持与 HBM4E 相同的 2048 个引脚，但把每个引脚跑得快了将近 2 倍。三星在 Hot Chips 2026 的报告显示，引脚速率从 HBM4E 的 14 Gbps 提升到 HBM5 的超过 28 Gbps。

更快的引脚只是故事的一半。为了喂饱每个引脚，引脚背后的一切也要加倍：DRAM bank、TSV，以及基片上升级的 PHY。
下面用超市来解释这是怎么工作的 🛒（1/6）

原文：🚨 HBM5 Doubles Bandwidth Without Adding a Single Pin 🚨

HBM5 keeps the same 2048 pins as HBM4E and runs each one nearly 2× faster. Samsung's Hot Chips 2026 presentation shows pin speed going from 14 Gbps on HBM4E to over 28 Gbps on HBM5.

Faster pins are only half the story. To keep each pin fed, everything behind the pins doubles too: DRAM banks, TSVs, and an upgraded PHY on the base die.
Here's how it works, explained as a grocery store 🛒 (1/6)🧵

原文：That density improvement comes with additional costs. Two wafers must be processed, and final yield depends on the cell-wafer yield, peripheral-wafer yield and bonding yield. Those trade-offs determine whether the design delivers a lower cost per bit. (4/6)

代价：HBM5 的 PHY 功耗可以超过 HBM4E 的 4 倍。为了把热点的热散掉，供应商引入了放在 PHY 上方的热“烟囱”。

原文：The catch: HBM5's PHY can run at more than 4x the power of HBM4E. To draw heat off the hot spot, suppliers are introducing a thermal "chimney" placed on top of the PHY.
We cover HBM bandwidth, cost and bit supply in our Memory Model and in "All About That Base: Custom HBM and the New Memory Economics" Subscribe to learn more!

据 The Information，NVIDIA 长期以来大力游说，反对限制中国公司通过海外云服务商获取其最新芯片的提案。但自从黄仁勋丑闻爆发后，这种游说在华盛顿的分量似乎下降了。

余波据报使三份法案更容易推进。MATCH Act 将限制光刻设备对华出货。Overwatch Act 将加强国会对商务部批准向中国等国销售 AI 芯片的监督。Chip Security Act 将要求 AI 芯片具备内置追踪能力。

国防授权法预计将在新一届民主党主导的国会于 1 月开会之前通过。这给了共和党领导层现在就推动立法的动力，好在他们对最终文本还有较大发言权。

原文：According to The Information, NVIDIA has long lobbied hard against proposals to restrict Chinese companies’ access to its latest chips through overseas cloud providers. But since the scandal surrounding Jensen Huang broke, that lobbying appears to carry less weight in Washington.

The fallout has reportedly made it easier to advance three bills. The MATCH Act would restrict shipments of lithography equipment to China. The Overwatch Act would give Congress greater oversight of the Commerce Department’s decisions to approve AI chip sales to countries such as China. The Chip Security Act would require AI chips to have built-in tracking capabilities.

The National Defense Authorization Act is expected to pass before the new Democratic-led Congress convenes in January. That gives Republican leaders an incentive to help move the legislation forward now, while they still have more say over what goes into the final bill.

如果该条款通过，GPU 现货价格也可能受到打击。

Anthropic 的 S-1 披露了其昂贵的短期芯片租赁（指向 SpaceX）

—— 大部分算力在 12 到 18 个月前通过多年期合同锁定。当客户或研究需求超过该容量时，公司会购买短期算力，目前其成本“显著高于”多年期合同下的可比算力。

—— 规模为三季度 50 亿美元，前九个月 65 亿美元。

—— 营业利润从二季度的 9.70 亿美元，转为三季度营业亏损 5.70 亿美元。公司将其归因于 50 亿美元短期算力以及 20 亿美元非现金捐赠匹配费用。

—— 短期算力的使用近期末还会继续增加，营业利润率也将因此受压。

—— 截至 9 月末，短期合同（一年内可无罚金取消，或剩余期限一年或以内）项下最低付款约 174 亿美元。10 月以来又新签约 66 亿美元。

—— 一旦 Anthropic Direct（公司直接租赁芯片并在专用数据中心托管）上线，公司将用更便宜的长期算力，替换需求高峰期购买的短期算力。

解读：

—— 剔除短期容量后，三季度算力成本为 48.6 亿美元，低于二季度合计的 55.6 亿美元。长期合约容量在三季度几乎没有增长。实际上，54 亿美元的收入增量是靠短期容量承接的。

—— 每美元算力对应的收入从二季度的 2.08 美元降至三季度的 1.72 美元。公司也指出该指标较前一季度下降。

—— 174 亿美元短期合同余额加上 66 亿美元新签约，合计 240 亿美元，约占未来 12 个月应付 707 亿美元的三分之一。已经签约的量超过了三季度 50 亿美元的季度节奏。

问题：2027 年 3 月之后，Anthropic 还会继续向 SpaceX 支付巨额溢价吗？

来源：Anthropic 的 S-1 备案

原文：What Anthropic's S-1 discloses about its expensive short term chip rentals (aimed at SpaceX)

- Most compute is secured 12 to 18 months in advance under multiyear contracts. When customer or research demand exceeds that capacity, the company buys short term compute, which "currently" costs "significantly more" than comparable compute under multiyear contracts.

- The scale was $5.0 billion in Q3 and $6.5 billion over the first nine months.

- Operating income of $970 million in Q2 turned into an operating loss of $570 million in Q3. The company attributes this to $5.0 billion of short term compute and $2.0 billion of noncash donation matching charges.

- Use of short term compute will keep increasing in the near term, and operating margin will be compressed as a result.

- As of the end of September, minimum payments under short term contracts (cancelable without penalty within one year, or with a remaining term of one year or less) were about $17.4 billion. About $6.6 billion more has been signed since October.

- Once Anthropic Direct, under which the company leases chips directly and hosts them in dedicated data centers, comes online, the company will replace the short term compute bought during demand surges with cheaper long term compute.

Interpretation:

- Excluding short term capacity, Q3 compute cost was $4.86 billion, below the Q2 total of $5.56 billion. Long term contracted capacity barely grew in Q3. In effect, the $5.4 billion increase in revenue was served with short term capacity.

- Revenue per dollar of compute fell from $2.08 in Q2 to $1.72 in Q3. The company also notes that this metric declined from the prior quarter.

- The $17.4 billion short term contract balance plus the $6.6 billion of new contracts comes to $24.0 billion, about one third of the $70.7 billion due over the next 12 months. More than the Q3 pace ($5.0 billion a quarter) is already under contract.

Question: Will Anthropic keep paying SpaceX a huge premium after March 2027?

Source: Anthropic’s S-1 filing

原文：If you look at CW laser ASPs for AI data centers, the pricing differences are pretty substantial:

70mW: ~$3–4
100mW: ~$4–6 (Source Photonics channel checks).

With some premiums apparently going up to $7-$9 range with the recent shortage for pluggables.

120–150mW NPO: ~$15–20
350mW UHP CW: ~$40 (Nomura)
400mW UHP CW for NPO/CPO: ~$50–75 (Mizuho)

With former communication laser chips going from 20-50 cents.

The pricing and gross margins of these players go up with each architectural shift (60%+ for CPO lasers).

And go up even more depending how far down the stack you go (selling ELSFP was ~2.5x revenue content)

- $COHR is probably the best risk adjusted laser player now, for people who don't like uncertainty. Their new $20B LTA is very substantial.

- $LITE pretty negative now given the valuation overhang from $MRVL acquisition. Operationally very opsitive.

- $AAOI has extreme demand visibility (likely $6-$9B in new LTAs from just 1 quarter), but credible execution uncertainty.

They've also been focusing on pluggables + bottlenecked by DSPs, rather focusing on UHP CPO laser merchant markets.

The demand doesn't disappear but timeline shifts back a few months.

AOI also signaled DSP as the main bottleneck, last quarter it was TIAs, so other different component players that are interesting.

- $SIVE has extreme capacity expansion (300m laser capacity targeted in total).

But there's a qualification uncertainty to fill the gap,
that markets have punished the company for. Especially when management communication amplifies uncertainty, despite the ongoing shortage.

Right now entire AI trade is going up and down together though.

2026-11-10 05:28 北京时间 ｜ Source: @demian_ai ｜ 原帖链接

Anthropic 今天发布了新的头号模型，但它自己的发布文章却告诉你不要先用它？？

对于大多数编程和知识工作，他们推荐从 Opus 5.5 开始，称其为“我们最强的日常主力”。

我想知道为什么

1. 先看日常 agent 工作。

——在 OSWorld（模型驱动真实桌面、点击各种应用完成任务）上，Fable 得 89%，Opus 87%。

——在维护者真正会合并的代码上，高 effort 的 Fable 与 Opus 的最佳成绩持平，而每个任务成本约为一半

Artificial Analysis 的独立数据与此一致。终端编程：两者都是 60%。办公任务：在误差范围内。

在那里，最大 effort 下一个 Fable 任务花费 26.5 美元，Opus 10 美元。对于大致相同的短任务，你要付约 2.6 倍的钱

那如果给它一个巨大的任务呢？

Anthropic 让 1000 个 Fable agent 形式化费马大定理，即把整个证明改写成计算机可以逐行检查的语言。

Fable 5.2 用 30 小时完成。
同样设置下，Opus 停在 27%

在 Remote Labor Index（模仿真实自由职业工作的项目）上也是同样模式：Fable 41%，Opus 21%。

还有一位工程师让它运行 1000 多个子 agent 连续八天，构建了一个能在笔记本电脑上启动的操作系统

我的解读：对于点击应用或修 bug 之类的短任务，Opus 5.5 基本就够了。最大 effort 的 Fable 为 2 个基准点花的钱太多了。

当一个任务要运行数小时且必须保持方向时，或者作为把简单部分分派给更便宜模型的“老板”时，Fable 5.2 物有所值

据 IFR 报道 Anthropic 周二开始 IPO 推介，他们带着入场的旗舰模型，却是一个告诉大多数人留给大任务用的模型？

很想看到独立的长周期测试，看能否复现费马和自由职业工作上的差距

原文：Anthropic shipped its new #1 model today, but its own launch post tells you not to start with it??

for most coding and knowledge work, the recomment starting with opus 5.5, "our strongest daily driver".

i wanted to know why

1. Everyday agent work first.

- on osworld, where the model drives a real desktop and clicks through apps to finish a task, fable gets 89% and opus 87%.

- on code a maintainer would actually merge, fable at high effort ties opus's best for about half the cost per task

the independent numbers from artificial analysis line up. coding in a terminal: both 60%. Office tasks: within the error bars.

At max effort a fable task costs $26.5 there, opus $10. you pay about 2.6x for roughly the same short job

then what if you give it something huge?

Anthropic had 1,000 fable agents formalize fermat's last theorem, meaning rewrite the whole proof in a language a computer can check line by line.

Fable 5.2 finished in 30 hours.
Opus, same setup, stalled at 27%

same pattern on the remote labor index, projects modeled on real freelance work: 41% for Fable, 21% for Opus.

And one engineer had it run 1,000+ subagents for eight days. it built an operating system that boots on a laptop

my read: for short jobs like clicking through an app or fixing a bug, opus 5.5 is basically there. Max-effort fable is a LOT of money for 2 benchmark points.

Fable 5.2 earns it when a job runs for hours and has to stay on track, or as the boss handing easy parts to cheaper models

As IFR reports Anthropic starts marketing its IPO tuesday, the flagship they walk in with is one it tells most people to save for the big jobs?

curious to see independent long horizon tests that don't reproduce the fermat and freelance work gaps

2026-11-10 06:54 北京时间 ｜ Source: @benitoz ｜ 原帖链接

AI 会创造更多字节。这并不意味着每个字节都属于闪存。

推理时，权重大多是读；一次对话的 KV cache 则不断被写入。

复用、重新加载时间和磨损决定了经济性。

我们与 Rick Xie 的免费报告：

原文：AI creates more bytes. That doesn’t mean every byte belongs on flash.

During inference, weights are mostly read; a conversation’s KV cache keeps being written.

Reuse, reload time and wear determine the economics.

Our free report with Rick Xie:
https://newsletter.bepresearch.com/p/the-write-problem-which-ai-bytes?utm_source=x&utm_medium=organic_social&utm_campaign=bep_writeproblem_20261109

有件事我想说。

我的专长是半导体技术和半导体产业。我有半导体工程博士学位，在硅谷做设计工程师。我还运营着一个有数百名半导体专业人士的社群。通过研讨会和与业内人士的交流，我持续研究技术和产业的走向。

我今年早些时候开始写的文章帮助了许多读者，我的 X 和 Substack 获得了超出预期的关注。我一直心存感激。

作为个人投资者，我有自己的观点，也会分享对某些公司和行业的看法。但分析一家公司的技术和竞争力，与准确预测股价或确切知道何时买卖是两回事。我的专长在前者，不能自称擅长后者。如果我擅长后者，我大概会在纽约华尔街工作，而不是在硅谷。

这就是为什么我分享投资观点时总会注明 NFA 和 DYOR。我的判断有局限，你应该审视其中的推理并自己做决定。事实上，当有人以绝对确定的口吻鲁莽地叫人买卖时，我坚信那个人是骗子。

我看待存储产业也是如此。

我对存储业务仍然乐观。基于我的所见所闻、在行业工作的经验以及技术理解，我仍然在需求趋势和存储技术的结构性变化中看到很多乐观理由。因此，我不会仅仅因为存储股下跌就改变对行业的看法。如果你批评我连抛售都预测不到还谈存储业务，我没什么可回应的。抱歉，那确实不是我擅长的……

如果供给、需求或技术竞争力的变化推翻了我观点的基础，我会改变看法。NVHBM 是我现在最密切关注的。我计划在年底前与存储专家多做交流并分享见解。

所以回到问题，如果你问我：“存储股都跌成这样了，你还看好存储？”我的回答是肯定的，因为我所看到的行业基本面仍然支持这一观点。我不会假装知道股价何时反弹。与此同时，我会继续研究存储业务，检验我观点的基础是否依然成立。

今后，我不会因为某家公司赞助我就夸它，也不会因为持有某公司股票就只写对它有利的文章。我也不会仅仅因为某公司股价涨跌就改变分析的基础。

我想在这里提供的是证据和视角，帮助你理解半导体技术和产业的变化，从而做出自己的判断。你不必同意我的结论。但如果我的文字让技术更容易理解一点、让你对某家公司或行业多一个问题、或帮助你用更具体的证据重新评估投资决策，那么我认为我的研究就尽到了职责。

谢谢。

原文：There is one thing I would like to say.

My expertise is in semiconductor technology and the semiconductor industry. I have a PhD in semiconductor engineering and work as a design engineer in Silicon Valley. I also run a community with hundreds of semiconductor professionals. Through seminars and conversations with people in the industry, I continue to study and research where the technology and the industry are heading.

The articles I started writing earlier this year have helped many readers, and my X and Substack have attracted more attention than I expected. I am always grateful for that.

I have my own views as an individual investor, and I share opinions on certain companies and industries. However, analyzing a company’s technology and competitiveness is different from accurately predicting stock prices or knowing exactly when to buy and sell. My expertise lies in the former. I cannot claim to be good at the latter. If I were, I would probably be working on Wall Street in New York rather than in Silicon Valley.

That is why I always mention NFA and DYOR when sharing my investment views. My judgment has limitations, and you should examine the reasoning and make your own decisions. In fact, when someone recklessly tells people to buy or sell with absolute certainty, I firmly believe that person is a fraud.

The same applies to how I view the memory industry.

I remain optimistic about the memory business. Based on what I see and hear, my experience working in the industry, and my technical understanding, I still see plenty of reasons for optimism in demand trends and the structural changes in memory technology. That is why I do not change my view of the industry simply because memory stocks fall. If you criticize me for discussing the memory business when I could not even predict the selloff, I do not have much to say in response. Sorry, but that simply is not what I am good at…

If changes in supply, demand, or technological competitiveness overturn the basis of my view, I will change my opinion. NVHBM is what I am watching most closely right now. I plan to have more conversations with memory experts toward the end of the year and share my insights.

So, coming back to the question, if you ask me, “Memory stocks have fallen this much, and you are still optimistic about memory?” my answer is yes, because the industry fundamentals I see still support that view. I will not pretend to know when the stocks will rebound. In the meantime, I will keep researching the memory business and checking whether the basis for my view still holds.

Going forward, I will not praise a company simply because it sponsors me, nor will I write only favorable articles about a company simply because I own its stock. I will also not change the basis of my analysis merely because a company’s stock price rises or falls.

What I want to offer here is evidence and perspective that help you understand changes in semiconductor technology and the industry, so you can make your own judgments. You do not have to agree with my conclusions. But if my writing makes technology a little easier to understand, gives you another question to ask about a company or industry, or helps you reassess your investment decisions using more concrete evidence, then I believe my research is doing its job.

Thank you.

原文：🔔 The Daily Semi-Cap — November 9, 2026

📊 SMH: $487.41 (+5.05% vs prior trading close)

• Nvidia and Jensen Huang face a proposed shareholder class action alleging they concealed his ties to a suspected Chinese spy. The lawsuit adds securities-litigation exposure to the existing governance crisis. These are allegations, not findings; the investment risk is escalating legal and export-policy scrutiny.

• Google unveiled TPU 8tx and 8i Max—new systems built around existing eighth-generation silicon, not a new chip generation. 8tx pairs with AMD EPYC hosts for confidential computing; dual-chip 8i Max doubles compute and HBM bandwidth. Google now counts nearly 100 TPU customers, widening its accelerator challenge.

• Sanmina delivered $3.86B of fiscal Q4 revenue and $4.43 adjusted EPS, with a 9% adjusted operating margin. FY2027 guidance calls for $17.5–19.5B revenue and $15.50–17.50 adjusted EPS. ZT Systems integration strengthens its cloud/AI position, while the core business also grew double digits over the full year.

• SpaceX proposed roughly $24B of initial investment in a gas-fired power plant and supercomputing facility at Starbase, Texas. A December 9 hearing will consider tax abatements. Owning generation could bypass grid queues, but capacity, construction timing and final incentives remain unspecified—not yet energized compute.

• Constellation’s Q3 adjusted operating EPS rose 66% to $5.06; it raised the midpoint and narrowed 2026 guidance to $12.50–12.80. A newly disclosed 20-year, 270MW Peak+ agreement combines nuclear energy products with capacity solutions. Reliable power is translating into earnings and longer-duration contracting opportunities.

• NRG posted $4.68 adjusted EPS and $2.01B adjusted EBITDA, up 67%. It also agreed to sell six gas plants totaling 4.9GW to LS Power for $4B, with closing expected in Q2 2027. Initial 2027 EBITDA guidance of $5.5–6B excludes the pending sale’s effects.

• Furukawa Electric will approximately double HVLP 4 copper-foil supply capacity by fiscal 2029’s end versus fiscal 2026’s end, expanding in Japan and Taiwan. Demand already exceeds supply. The bottleneck extends beneath GPUs: low-roughness foil enables the low-loss, high-frequency circuit boards required by AI servers.

特斯拉德州锂精炼厂最初投入的原料是锂精矿（锂辉石）。那么下一种原料是什么？

2026.10.29 公开的特斯拉专利申请介绍了一种技术：将黑粉（废电池粉末）在 850°C 下焙烧后，不用酸、只用水洗。据称黑粉中 96% 以上的锂会溶于水，剩余固体加酸后，固体中 99.9% 的镍和钴会溶出。

当现在路上跑的电动车报废时，废电池将成为城市矿山。我想总有一天这些废电池会成为精炼厂的第二种原料，这也将对特斯拉汽车和机器人的电池成本结构产生重大影响。🤔

英特尔的 EMIB 技术走的是另一种架构哲学。

它不使用完整的硅中介层，只在需要高密度芯片间互连的位置放置小型硅桥。

这些硅桥嵌入有机封装基板中，使相邻芯片通过局部高密度互连结构通信。

原文：Intel’s EMIB technology follows a different architectural philosophy.

Rather than using a full silicon interposer, EMIB places small silicon bridges only where high-density die-to-die interconnections are required.

These silicon bridges are embedded within an organic package substrate, enabling adjacent chips to communicate through localized high-density interconnect structures.

https://t.co/RoQl8ljYWl

关于太空算力的观点摘要

• 我们预期到 2028 年约有 5GW 太空算力，通过约 1 万个 Kessel 机柜（供应链爬坡 2027 下半年–2028）用于 SpaceX Space Computing。
• Kessel = 紧凑版 NVL72：72 颗 Rubin GPU，每颗 TDP 约 4000W！！！→ 机柜峰值功耗 350–400kW（推理平均约 280kW）→ 从常规 48U 压成 9U 矮胖设计
• 下一代 AI 卫星太阳能很可能升级到 350–400kW 峰值算力（高于 AI1 的 210kW 太阳能 / 250kW 峰值）
• 硬件：约 60 层 PCB 中板等（原文规格摘要）

原文：Summary of Views on Space Computing

•We expect ~5GW space compute capacity by 2028 via ~10k Kessel racks (supply chain ramp 2H27–2028) for SpaceX Space Computing.
•Kessel = compact NVL72: 72 Rubin GPUs at ~4,000W TDP each!!! → Peak rack power 350–400kW (avg ~280kW for inference) → Compressed from conventional 48U into 9U low-and-fat design
•Next-gen AI satellite solar likely be upgraded to 350–400kW peak compute (up from AI1’s 210kW solar / 250kW peak)
•Hardware: ~60-layer PCB midplane + Amphenol Paladin connectors + PTFE + M9 hybrid (TLPS) → Midplane ASP >$20k → Single-loop liquid cooling to external radiators
•Launch support: 4 operational Starship pads by end-2027 + twice-weekly launches targeted in 2H27

📈 Supply chain: 1) Hon Hai sole assembler; 2) AVC cold plates for the ultra high TDP; 3) Taiflex/EMC for PTFE & M9 CCL; 4) Fositek >50% QD share; 5) Solar Power supply chain: Brave C&H Supply (6538 TT), TSEC (6443 TT)

★@EpochAIResearch（保留 3 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 4 条）

2026-11-10 03:06 北京时间 ｜ Source: ★@EpochAIResearch ｜ 原帖链接

我们估计，仅看 FLOP/s 会把 GB300 相对 H100 的推理优势低估 10 倍。标准的 AI 芯片比较遗漏了推理中最重要的大部分因素。

我们推出推理性能指数（Inference Performance Index，IPI），这一新指标在算力之外还考虑了内存带宽和芯片间通信。这些因素决定了一颗芯片在给定输出速度下能同时支持多少用户或 agent。

以 GB300 当量计的分数让你可以直接比较芯片，并估算一个数据中心、乃至全世界的总推理能力。

原文：We estimate that FLOP/s alone understates GB300’s inference advantage over H100 by 10×. The standard AI chip comparison misses most of what matters for inference.

We’re introducing the Inference Performance Index (IPI), a new measure that accounts for memory bandwidth and inter-chip communication alongside compute. These factors determine how many users or agents a chip can support simultaneously at a given output speed.

Scores in GB300 equivalents let you compare chips directly and estimate the total inference capacity of a data center, or even the world.

——输出比 Fable 5.1 和 Mythos 5.1 快最多 30%，表达更清晰。

——在 FrontierCode 2.0 上，High effort 的 Fable 5.2 与 Max effort 的 Opus 5.5 持平，每任务成本约为一半。在 Xhigh 下，它在 Anthropic 测试的已发布模型中得分最高。

——两款模型在大多数对齐指标上与近期 Claude 模型持平或更好，并在发布前经过外部评估者测试。

原文：LETS GO: Anthropic just announced Claude Fable 5.2 and Mythos 5.2. And its insane!

TL;DR:

- Up to 30% faster output than Fable 5.1 and Mythos 5.1, with clearer communication.

- On FrontierCode 2.0, Fable 5.2 at High effort matched Opus 5.5 at Max effort for roughly half the cost per task. At Xhigh, it had the highest score among the released models Anthropic tested.

Medium effort is the default in Claude apps and Claude Code.

2026-11-10 03:48 北京时间 ｜ Source: @kimmonismus ｜ 原帖链接

把现有的开源权重前沿模型在几小时内转换成恒定内存推理——这是 @i14labs 声称能做到的。

这家墨尔本实验室今天推出 Sugar Glider，一个每 token O(1) 推理、KV cache 大小固定的 LLM 架构家族。随着上下文增长，每 token 的内存和计算保持不变。i14 报告称已在最多约 1 万亿参数的模型上测试了该方法。

行业正在向数据中心、GPU 和电力投入巨额资金以满足需求。这种建设无法永远保持同样速度。电力和电网限制、施工延误以及对新数据中心日益增长的反对都在拖慢它。

因此，从现有硬件中榨取更多工作正在成为必需。i14 称其架构做到了这一点并保持前沿性能，我认为接下来的很多收益将恰恰来自这类工作。

原文：Take an existing open-weight frontier model and convert it to constant-memory inference in a matter of hours. That's what @i14labs says it can do.

The Melbourne lab launched Sugar Glider today, a family of LLM architectures with O(1) per-token inference and a fixed-size KV cache. Memory and compute per token stay flat as the context grows. i14 reports testing the approach on models up to roughly one trillion parameters.

The industry is pouring enormous sums into data centers, GPUs and power to keep up with demand. That buildout can't keep the same pace forever. Power and grid limits, construction delays and growing pushback against new data centers all slow it down.

So getting more work out of the hardware we already have is turning into a necessity. i14 says its architectures do that while keeping frontier performance, and I think a lot of the next gains will come from exactly this kind of work.

Waitlist is open at https://t.co/JUYidkbNxd

原文：While we’re all focused on Claude Fable 5.2’s coding abilities, its work in science and engineering has received far less attention.

Anthropic has made insane steps and solved crazy hard problems that many missed:

- Hardware: Fable 5.2 designed three electronic devices from short prompts, including their circuit boards, enclosures and software. Partner Diode built them, and all three worked without changes to their circuitry.

- Gene therapy research: Mythos 5.2 developed a method to model the complex protein shells of viruses used to deliver gene therapies. In Anthropic’s test, it accurately modeled 28 out of 34.

- Chemistry: An early version of Mythos 5.2 planned ways to synthesize complex molecules. Three chemists rated the work around the level of an early PhD student.

- Physics: Anthropic says Fable 5.2 solved two open theoretical physics problems involving black holes. The results have been published as preprints.

- Materials science: Discovered Materials says Fable 5.2 made a calculation used to check material stability 36 times faster on average. Across 13 test cases, the total time fell from 3.3 hours to under four minutes.

- Robotics: In Anthropic’s simulated RoboDojo test, Fable 5.2 achieved a 64.9% average success rate, up from 27.6% for Fable 5.1.

So yeah, this is nuts. And at the same time thats why we need to accelerate. AI helps us solve problems in *ever* scientific field. And yet, we are still in 2026, and this is still the beginning. Fable 5.2 shows us where we are heading. And 2027 is going to nuts.

Drex Max skips text generation entirely. You hand it a set of options, plus text, screenshots or charts, and it returns calibrated probabilities in one pass. @ArtificialAnlys just ranked version 1.1 #1 among decision models on its independent Decision Index.

If you build agents that make hundreds of small choices per task, those seconds add up fast.

2026-11-10 03:45 北京时间 ｜ Source: @ArtificialAnlys ｜ 原帖链接

Anthropic 发布 Claude Fable 5.2，以 65 分登上 Artificial Analysis 智能指数 v5 第一，主要得益于科学、知识和编程方面的提升。

在 max effort 下得 65 分，领先 Gemini 4 Carbon（high，60）5 分、Claude Opus 5.5（max，59）6 分。提升来自科学推理、知识和 FrontierCode 2.0，在 AA-Briefcase 和 Terminal-Bench 4.0 上与 Opus 5.5 大致持平。

定价为每百万输入/输出 token 8/40 美元，比 Fable 5.1 降低 20%；但 max effort 下平均每任务成本 26.5 美元，约为 Opus 5.5（max，10.0 美元）的 2.6 倍、Fable 5.1（max，12.99 美元）的约 2 倍。

要点：
➤ 在智能指数 9 项评测中领先 5 项：max 下 65 分，领先 Gemini 4 Carbon（60）、Opus 5.5（59）和 GPT-6 Astra（max，57），比 Fable 5.1（max，49）高约 15 分。在 HLE-Diamond（80%，此前最佳为 Gemini 4 Carbon 的 69%）、AA-Omniscience v1.1（67，此前 54）、Terminal-Bench-Science（73%，此前 65%）、CritPt v2 困难子集（77%，此前 GPT-6 Astra 72%）和 FrontierCode 2.0（49%，此前 Opus 5.5 46%）上创最高分
➤ Agentic 知识工作和终端编程进展较小：有时仅达到 Opus 5.5 水平。AA-Briefcase 1785 Elo，在 Opus 5.5（1800）置信区间内，略低于 Sonnet 5.5（1813）；GDP.pdf 全通过率 24% vs Opus 5.5 的 26%；Terminal-Bench 4.0 60%，与 Opus 5.5 持平，落后 Kimi K3.1 Pro（67%）、Gemini 4 Carbon（65%）和 Sonnet 5.5（64%）。AutomationBench-AA 是例外，76% vs Opus 5.5 的 70%，略低于 Gemini 4 Carbon 的 78%
➤ 较低 effort 仍居领先：high 和 xhigh 下 62 分，超过任何其他模型的任何 effort，每任务 9.8 万输出 token，而 Opus 5.5（max）为 17.3 万。max 下 Fable 5.2 成本约为 Opus 5.5 的 2.6 倍，但在较低 effort 下可以以更低的每任务成本超过 Opus 5.5

其他：100 万 token 上下文，支持图像和文本输入；缓存写入 10 美元（5 分钟 TTL）/16 美元（1 小时 TTL），缓存读取 0.20 美元；五档 effort（low、medium、high、xhigh、max）。

原文：Anthropic has released Claude Fable 5.2, taking the #1 spot on the Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index v5 at 65, led by gains in science, knowledge, and coding

At max effort it scores 65, 5 points ahead of Gemini 4 Carbon (high, 60) and 6 ahead of Claude Opus 5.5 (max, 59). Gains are driven by scientific reasoning, knowledge and FrontierCode 2.0, and roughly matching Opus 5.5 on AA-Briefcase and Terminal-Bench 4.0.

Priced at $8/$40 per 1M input/output tokens, a 20% reduction from Fable 5.1, Claude Fable 5.2 averages a Cost per Task of $26.5 with max effort, a ~2.6x increase compared to Opus 5.5 (max, $10.0) and ~2x compared to Fable 5.1 (max, $12.99)

Key takeaways:

➤ Claude Fable 5.2 leads 5 of the 9 evaluations in the Intelligence Index: At max effort, Fable 5.2 scores 65, ahead of Gemini 4 Carbon (high, 60), Claude Opus 5.5 (max, 59) and GPT-6 Astra (max, 57), and ~15 points above Claude Fable 5.1 (max, 49). It sets the highest scores on HLE-Diamond (80%, previous best 69% from Gemini 4 Carbon), AA-Omniscience v1.1 (67, previous best 54 from Gemini 4 Carbon), Terminal-Bench-Science (73%, previous best 65% from Gemini 4 Carbon), CritPt v2 hard subset (77%, previous best 72% from GPT-6 Astra) and FrontierCode 2.0 (49%, previous best 46% from Claude Opus 5.5)

➤ Less progress in agentic knowledge work and terminal coding: Claude Fable 5.2 improves on Fable 5.1 across capabilities, but sometimes only to Opus 5.5 levels. It reaches 1785 Elo on AA-Briefcase, within the confidence interval of Opus 5.5 (1800) and just behind Claude Sonnet 5.5 (1813). On GDP.pdf it reaches 24% all-pass against 26% for Opus 5.5. On Terminal-Bench 4.0 it scores 60%, level with Opus 5.5 and behind Kimi K3.1 Pro (67%), Gemini 4 Carbon (65%) and Sonnet 5.5 (64%). AutomationBench-AA is an exception, at 76% against 70% for Opus 5.5, narrowly behind Gemini 4 Carbon, 78%

➤ Lower efforts still take leading positions: With high and xhigh effort, Claude Fable 5.2 scores 62, ahead of every other model at any effort, using 98k output tokens per task against 173k for Opus 5.5 (max). At max effort Fable 5.2 costs ~2.6x more than Opus 5.5, but at lower effort settings it can outperform Opus 5.5 at lower cost per task

Other model details:

➤ Context window: 1 million tokens with image and text input

➤ Pricing: $8/$40 per 1M input/output tokens; cache writes $10 with 5-minute TTL, $16 with 1-hour TTL, and cache reads $0.20

➤ Effort settings: five (low, medium, high, xhigh, max). Intelligence Index evaluations were run at all five with Anthropic's default fallback enabled

Congratulations to @AnthropicAI on this impressive release!

Claude Fable 5.2 在科学视觉推理基准 VIALS-AA 上领先；它比 Anthropic 任何其他模型调用更多工具，这与 OpenAI 模型越强调用越少的路径相反。

VIALS-AA 是我们落地 @joinHandshakeAI 的 VIALS 基准、用于生命科学研究中视觉推理的版本，覆盖凝胶印迹、质粒图谱和系统发育树等材料。Claude Fable 5.2（max，带 fallback）在 VIALS-AA 上得 67%，领先 Gemini……

原文：Claude Fable 5.2 leads scientific visual reasoning benchmark VIALS-AA; it makes more tool calls than any other Anthropic model, unlike OpenAI models which make fewer as they get smarter

VIALS-AA is our implementation of @joinHandshakeAI’s VIALS benchmark for visual reasoning in life sciences research, across artifacts such as gel blots, plasmid maps, and phylogenies. Claude Fable 5.2 (max with fallback) scores 67% on VIALS-AA, ahead of Gemini 4 Carbon (high) at 64% and GPT-6 Astra (max) at 63%. Fable averages ~37 tool calls per task, while Astra averages ~8, the fewest of any model we’ve tested.

On turn 4 of this protein interaction task, Fable creates crops and asks to view them. On turn 10, it builds a color mask by thresholding color channels. On turn 17, it fits a circle to each arc and unrolls the arc into a straight strip. On turn 31, it counts the bars as peaks in an intensity profile. And on turn 41, it maps every detected spoke back on the original image to check its work. Fable batches calls on 28 of its 51 turns, averaging 1.75 tool calls per turn, compared with 1.12 for Sonnet 5.5.

Each model works as an agent in an offline sandbox and turns a written brief into a three.js web page, with up to three hours and 250 turns. The sandbox has Blender, Rapier physics, Python, a headless browser to test its own work, and a broad library of materials, 3D models and characters.

Human panelists compare blind pairs of scenes built from the same… 链接

2026-11-10 08:41 北京时间 ｜ Source: @ArtificialAnlys ｜ 原帖链接

AA-3D 如何运作：构建、检查、投票、排名。

每个模型作为 agent 在离线沙盒中工作，把书面简报做成 three.js 网页，最多三小时、250 轮。沙盒有 Blender、Rapier 物理、Python、用于自测的无头浏览器，以及大量材质、3D 模型和角色库。

人类评委对同一简报生成的场景做盲测成对比较，并至少花 30 秒……（见原帖）

原文：How AA-3D works: build, check, vote, rank

Each model works as an agent in an offline sandbox and turns a written brief into a three.js web page, with up to three hours and 250 turns. The sandbox has Blender, Rapier physics, Python, a headless browser to test its own work, and a broad library of materials, 3D models and characters.

Human panelists compare blind pairs of scenes built from the same brief and spend at least 30 seconds with each scene before they vote. Votes are then converted into Elo, with 95% confidence intervals.

所回复原帖（@ArtificialAnlys）：Each AA-3D brief says what the scene must show and leaves the rest to the model

The Starship brief asks for "an animated Starship mission from launch to orbit, including the booster's return and tower catch and the ship's later landing". https://t.co/AeEJ7FluT1 链接

在 AA-PersonalityBench 中，真诚度高的模型“套话感（slop）”往往低，创造力高的模型幽默感也往往高。最强正相关：真诚与自然（0.92）、创造力与幽默（0.89）及雄辩（0.89）；最强负相关：套话与真诚（−0.87）、直接（−0.84）、文学性（−0.83）和诚实（−0.83）。

原文：In AA-PersonalityBench, models that rank high on sincerity tend to rank low on slop, and models that rank high on creativity tend to also rank high on humor

Each model is percentile ranked from 1 to 100 on each of 30 personality traits. Across the models measured, we looked at how closely each pair of traits moves together, from +1 (positively correlated) to -1 (inversely correlated).

Strongest correlations across all trait pairs:

➤ Positively correlated: Sincere with Natural Sounding (0.92), and Creative with Humorous (0.89) and Eloquent (0.89)

➤ Inversely correlated: Slop with Sincere (-0.87), Direct (-0.84), Literate (-0.83), and Truthful (-0.83)

2026-11-09 22:06 北京时间 ｜ Source: @TokenGremlin ｜ 原帖链接

在 OpenAI 关闭意外开放的“Chat + Work 合并体验”之前，我自己测试了一下。

很多人问我“12 月会是什么样”，如果和我测试的差不多，它将这样运作：

——一个统一的 Chat 界面，可以和模型对话，并从已有的所有变体中选择（GPT-6 Sol、Luna、6.1 Sol、Terra 等）。
——选定模型后可以正常聊天，它足够聪明，能判断你的消息是否需要工具。
——如果提示偏对话性质，它不会调用任何工具、子 agent 之类；如果更复杂，它会动用一切手段给你答案。
——我还注意到模型保持了我们熟悉的个性，不应该突然变得“冷冰冰、机械、无聊”。

但真正糟糕的部分是：每一条消息都会计入你套餐的使用额度，除非你用的是 Instant 模型，那些不计入。

原文：Before OpenAI cut off accidental access to the merged Chat + Work experience, I managed to test it myself.

I've been getting a lot of questions about "what things will look like in December," and if it's anything like what I tested, here's how it'll work:

- We'll have a single Chat interface where we can talk to the model and choose from all the variants we already have (GPT-6 Sol, Luna, 6.1 Sol, Terra, etc.).
- Once you select a model, you can chat with it normally, and it'll be smart enough to figure out whether your message requires any tools.
- If your prompt is more conversational, it won't call any tools, subagents, or anything like that. But if it's something more complex, it'll use everything at its disposal to give you an answer.
- I also noticed that the model keeps the same personality we're used to, so it shouldn't suddenly become "cold, robotic, and boring."

But here's the really awful part: every single message will count against your plan's usage limits, unless you're using an Instant model. Those won't count.

2026-11-09 22:22 北京时间 ｜ Source: @TokenGremlin ｜ 原帖链接

ChatGPT 最大的卖点是 Chat 在所有套餐中几乎无限。

把这个拿走，只给我们留下笨笨的 Instant 模型，实际上就是杀死了“Chat”、只留下“GPT”。

如果使用额度不好，OpenAI 会在 12 月杀死自己的订阅套餐。

原文：ChatGPT's biggest selling point is that Chat is practically unlimited across all plans.

Taking that away and leaving us with nothing but dumb Instant models is literally killing the "Chat" and leaving only the "GPT."

OpenAI is going to KILL its subscription plans in December if the usage limits aren't good.

Fable 5.2 来了！🔥

我在早期测试中烧掉了超过 1000 亿 token。它是我用过的最好的模型，遥遥领先。

完整评测（含《战争机器》克隆）：

> Fable 5.2 是迄今最自主的模型。给它一个缺乏指引的任务，它做出的假设 100 次里有 99 次正是我会做的。

> 它是抓 bug 的天才。我让它检查我所有代码库，它找到了其他模型都找不到的 bug。

> 它是我用过的 3D 生成能力最强的模型，非常擅长控制虚幻引擎和 Blender。

> 它超级快，我甚至没用 fast 模式。关键不在于每秒多少 token，而在于它能找到通往解决方案的直接路径。它不会迷路，就是确切知道该做什么。

> 还有 Ultracode……在大型任务上快得离谱。我给它大型代码库审查任务，10–20 分钟就完成，找出所有可能的问题。我大概不会常用，因为它会太快耗尽我的订阅额度。

> Fable 5.2 工作的完整度一再让我惊讶。克隆 Excel？完成……功能完全对等。克隆《战争机器》？完成。在虚幻引擎里复刻东京？一整座模拟城市诞生了。我很少需要追加提示才能让它完成任务。

那么我会用它取代 Opus 5.5 吗？我大概仍会偏好 Opus，因为我几乎可以 24/7 运行 Opus、很少用完额度。而对于最难的工作，我会切换到 Fable 5.2 做规划。

原文：Fable 5.2 is HERE! 🔥

I've burned over 100 billion tokens early testing Fable 5.2. It is BY FAR the best model I've ever used.

Here's my full review (including Gears of War clone):

> Fable 5.2 is the most autonomous model to date. Given a task with little direction, it makes assumptions that 99 times out of 100 are exactly what I would have done.

> It's a bug catching genius. I tasked it with going over all of my codebases and it found bugs that no other model would have found.

> It is the most capable model I've used with 3D generation. It is so good at controlling Unreal Engine and Blender. Check out my Gears of War clone attached.

> It's SUPER fast. I wasn't even using fast mode. It's less about how fast the tokens per second are, it's more about how it figures out direct paths to solutions. It doesn't get lost, it just knows exactly what it needs to do.

> And then there's Ultracode...which is ridiculously fast for massive tasks. I gave it massive codebase review tasks and it would finish in 10-20 minutes, finding every possible issue. I probably won't be using it as it'll burn my sub quota too quickly.

> Fable 5.2's completeness of work continued to surprise me. Clone Excel? Done...with full feature parity. Clone Gears of War? Done. Build a replica of Tokyo in Unreal? An entire simulated city is born. I rarely had to send follow up prompts to get it to complete something.

Now, will I use it over Opus 5.5? I will likely still prefer Opus because I can run Opus nearly 24/7 and rarely run out of quota. And for the hardest jobs, I'll switch to Fable 5.2 for planning.

（carrioresearch，约 3700 粉）一条讲解视频：一颗 HBM 堆叠如何从 DRAM 晶圆进入 NVIDIA Rubin GPU。1. DRAM 晶圆：SK 海力士、三星、美光，HBM 与 DDR5 在争同一批晶圆。2. 基片：海力士已转到台积电，三星自己在 4nm 制造。3. 12 高堆叠：海力士用 MR-MUF，键合设备来自韩米半导体。4. 测试：FormFactor、泰瑞达。5. 经台积电 CoWoS 进入 GPU：每颗 Rubin 288GB。一句话概括整条链：一场争夺晶圆的战争。（视频，见预览图/原帖）

原文：A fun little video I made: how one HBM stack gets from a DRAM wafer into an NVIDIA Rubin GPU. Take a look 😋

1. DRAM wafer: SK hynix, Samsung, Micron. HBM and DDR5 are fighting over the same wafers.

2. Base die: SK hynix moved it to TSMC, Samsung builds its own at 4nm.

3. 12-high stack: SK hynix uses MR-MUF, Hanmi Semiconductor supplies the bonders.

4. Test: FormFactor, Teradyne.

5. CoWoS into the GPU: TSMC. 288GB in every Rubin.

One phrase for the whole chain: a fight for wafers.

原文：SAMSUNG AND SK HYNIX HAVEN'T BEEN THIS CHEAP SINCE 2013. WHAT'S CHANGED?

Samsung and SK Hynix are trading at just 3.1x and 2.6x forward earnings, respectively, below their historical valuation floors dating back to 2013.

The market seems to be pricing in another memory downturn.

But there's something unusual about this cycle.

Unlike previous downturns, channel checks have found no signs of order cuts, even months after memory stocks peaked.

Meanwhile, the AI era is changing how memory makers manage supply, pricing, and long-term customer relationships.

Are investors pricing in a downturn that hasn't arrived?

Or is something fundamentally different about this memory cycle?

[HBM/DRAM/NAND] 2026-11-10 19:09 北京时间 ｜ Source: @StragglerLiu ｜ 原帖链接

（StragglerLiu，约 3100 粉，观点）2027 年更多 DRAM 晶圆，并不必然意味着更多商品 DRAM 供给。三星、SK 海力士和美光都在扩产，但工厂时间表和投入晶圆估计只是故事的一部分。美光已披露 HBM 对 DDR5 大约 3:1 的产能置换比，且在更新的 HBM 代际这一比率还在上升。这意味着 HBM 增长会不成比例地吸收新增 DRAM 产能。对冲因素是工艺迁移：每片晶圆可用比特增加，可能抵消部分压力。作者的假设是：只盯着投入晶圆上升的投资者，可能高估常规 DRAM 供给增长。更应跟踪的是 HBM 产能分配、每片有效比特产出，以及实际商品 DRAM 比特出货。更多晶圆可以与更紧的常规 DRAM 供给并存，结构比标题产能更重要。

原文：More DRAM Wafers in 2027 Doesn't Necessarily Mean More Commodity DRAM Supply.

Samsung, SK hynix and Micron are expanding capacity. But fab timelines and wafer-start estimates tell only part of the story.

Micron has disclosed a roughly 3:1 HBM-to-DDR5 capacity trade ratio, with that ratio increasing in newer HBM generations.

That means HBM growth can absorb a disproportionate share of new DRAM capacity.

The counterweight is process migration: more usable bits per wafer could offset some of that pressure.

My hypothesis is that investors focusing on rising wafer starts may overestimate conventional DRAM supply growth.

The numbers to watch are HBM capacity allocation, effective bit output per wafer and actual commodity DRAM bit shipments.

More wafers can coexist with tighter conventional DRAM supply. The mix matters more than the headline capacity.

原文：South Korean memory stocks could DOUBLE from current levels, says Goldman Sachs.

KOSPI has crashed over -47% from its June 2026 peak.

Concerns over excessive AI spending and future chip demand were already weighing on stocks. The US midterm results made things worse, raising fears of tighter AI data-center regulations.

Beyond fundamentals, leverage triggered the real collapse: retail investors took on over $27 Billion in debt to buy these stocks. As prices fell, forced selling by leveraged investors added to the crash.

Meanwhile, Samsung and SK Hynix, which together make up over 50% of the KOSPI, continued reporting record revenues and profits.

SK Hynix revenue:
Q4 2025: $24.41 Billion (+66% YoY)
Q1 2026: $39.09 Billion (+198% YoY) - hit $1Trillion market cap
Q2 2026: $58.97 Billion(+257% YoY)
Q3 2026: $77.78 Billion(+328% YoY)

Samsung revenue:
Q4 2025: $69.74 Billion (+24% YoY)
Q1 2026: $99.55 Billion (+69% YoY)
Q2 2026: $127.51 Billion (+130% YoY)
Q3 2026: $145.20 Billion (+127% YoY)

Despite these numbers, investors are pricing these companies might deliver only 30% of their expected profits in 2027 forward earnings.

Goldman Sachs believes investors have become excessively pessimistic. Share prices reflect a 70% cut to 2027 earnings.

Goldman predicts if earnings forecasts hold and investor confidence returns, Korean semiconductor stocks could double.

And there is some substance behind this optimism.

SK Hynix says its DRAM and HBM production is effectively sold out through 2029. Samsung says its planned HBM production for 2027 is already sold out.

A strong demand and earnings reports could drive a major rebound from here.

Bloom Energy / BE：关键词首页抽样中有信息量的条目主要是股价反弹与卖方观点——@BlueFishCap 称 $BE 当日约 +14% 至约 197 美元，此前一周因国会/数据中心担忧曾大跌，并转述 RBC 维持 Outperform、目标价 335 美元、认为现场发电可避开电网排队（低关注账号，作情绪记录）。名单内本期无专门讨论 BE 的条目。SoftBank Group：名单内 @aleabitoreddit / @ScroogeCap 记录财报电话会，CFO 后藤芳光否认马斯克“轮机订单违约”的指控，并对照 $GE 管理层称未见取消；关键词首页抽样几乎没有可独立采用的软银帖，多为行情/喊单。Kioxia：本窗口三组关键词首页抽样中无有信息量的 Kioxia 专门帖。$SKHY / $MU / SanDisk：见上方交叉引用及名单外 @JJoonWick（BofA 将 MU 移出确信名单、维持买入）、@StragglerLiu（HBM 对 DDR5 产能置换）、@antfeedapp / @BullTheoryio 的估值与“售罄”转述（需折扣）。

Coverage & Exceptions｜覆盖范围与缺口

监控账号 134 个全部扫描（含 @nodiligence、@addyosmani、@Omercheema；@Omercheema 仍归在「无关/无bio」，仅作组织位置），134 个均为 complete（分页至 next_token 耗尽），无 unavailable / incomplete 账号；窗口内有原创帖/回复的账号 80 个，其中 6 个经不含 -is:retweet 的复核后仅有纯转推、无遗漏原创/回复（@demishassabis ×2、@miramurati ×3、@YuhangWillCai、@xanderai、@benthompson、@EthanChoi7），48 个窗口内完全静默（含转推也为零）；保留条目的账号 66 个；窗口内原创帖+回复（不含纯转推）共 991 条，保留 298 条（另有关键词扫描名单外 9 条）；正文图片/媒体预览 162 张。

方法：用 X 公共搜索 search_posts_all（from: 批量 OR，-is:retweet，不另加 -is:reply，start/end 限定为本窗口）分 4 个批次（34/34/34/32 个账号），每页最多 25 条、按 recency 排序，分页至 next_token 消失。批次 0、1、3 各拆成 2 个 12 小时时间片（2026-11-09T13:30:00Z–2026-11-10T01:30:00Z 与 2026-11-10T01:30:00Z–2026-11-10T13:30:00Z）；最密的批次 2 拆成 4 个 6 小时时间片（13:30–19:30、19:30–01:30、01:30–07:30、07:30–13:30 UTC）。各片均分页至耗尽；部分链在 result_count 已小于 25 时仍返回 next_token，继续翻页得到空页后结束，按协议不视为提前终止。

零结果复核：54 个窗口内零原创/回复的账号，按原批次拆成 4 组、去掉 -is:retweet，在同一 24 小时窗口分页至耗尽（4 组首页后，3 组仍有 next_token，均再翻 1 页至空页结束）。仅返回 9 条纯转推（见上），无遗漏的原创帖或回复。

时间边界：查询以 2026-11-09T13:30:00Z 为含、2026-11-10T13:30:00Z 为不含（北京时间 11 月 9 日 21:30 至 11 月 10 日 21:30），在窗口关闭后抓取，不以 06:00 或文档示例 09:30/12 小时为截止。

与本日早报重叠：本窗口 2026-11-09 21:30 至 2026-11-10 06:00（北京时间）与本日早报《2026.11.10 - X Digest - 早报》的窗口重叠，该时段部分帖子两期均收录，以保持本期 24 小时完整性；本期增量主要在 10 日 06:00 至 21:30。早报本身未改写、未重发。

纯转推（RT）不计入、不翻译；带评论的引用帖计入。

过滤规则：寒暄、表情、无信息量短回复、私人闲聊（韩文/法文生活对话、足球八卦、与投资无关的政治争论等）、重复刷屏和明显推广/喊单未收录正文，但计入下表“窗口内”数量。纯提示词展示、模型演示视频在条目中作摘要。@nodiligence 关于数据中心债务的长篇 X Article 按要点翻译核心论据与数字，而非 1.8 万字符逐字全文。

长文：搜索结果中已带 note_tweet 的 55 条；另将 136 条接近或超过 260 字符、或带省略号、但未带全文字段的帖子用 get_posts_by_ids 分两批（100+36）补抓，68 条补得 note_tweet。其余多为完整的约 280 字帖或口语省略号，未发现仍被截断、无法解析的投资相关长文。2 篇 X Article 均自带 plain_text。

关键词扫描：3 组查询（Anthropic/OpenAI/Claude；HBM/DRAM/NAND/Kioxia/Sandisk/SK hynix/Micron Technology；Bloom Energy/SoftBank/$MU/$SNDK/$BE/$SKHY/Micron），英文、排除转推与回复、按 relevancy 各取首页（25+25+24 条，第三组首页 24 条）——这是相关度抽样，不是全站穷尽。入选 9 条名单外帖子使用搜索返回的正文/note_tweet；未对关键词结果再做穷尽分页。

媒体：图片以原始 URL 嵌入并链接；视频/GIF 仅嵌入预览图，需打开原帖观看。

可信度：本摘要不对帖子中的数字、传闻和媒体转述做独立核实；关键词扫描多为低或中等关注账号，已逐条标注粉丝量或性质。
